2026-10-24T18:32:13.9435300Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9435427Z     def run(self):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9435785Z         """Run the installation task."""
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9436243Z         lang = self._get_supported_language()

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9437060Z

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9437667Z         """Return a supported locale.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9437940Z

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9438821Z         if self._is_language_support_installed(self._lang):

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9441347Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9441552Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9442303Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9442671Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9443104Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9443840Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9444252Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9444458Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9445104Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9445705Z         """
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9445998Z         try:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9446449Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9447023Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9447503Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9448018Z             return True
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9448225Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9448481Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9448869Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9449093Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9449608Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9449852Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9450039Z     def _write_language_configuration(self, lang):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9450630Z         """Write language configuration to the /etc/locale.conf file.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9451029Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9451244Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9451925Z         """

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9557773Z         if not self._localed_proxy:

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9558849Z         variants = []
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9559213Z         parsing_failed = False
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9559488Z

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9564364Z

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9564943Z             return

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9610092Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9610441Z

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9611343Z     """
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9611493Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9611856Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9612192Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9612433Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9612587Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9613001Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9613254Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9613435Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9614229Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9614584Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9614750Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9615392Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9615872Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9616196Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9616912Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9617687Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9618419Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9619113Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9619879Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9620502Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9620952Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9621459Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9622073Z             #
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9622390Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9623023Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9623650Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9624276Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9624929Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9625415Z             #
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9625904Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9626350Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9626588Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9627489Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9628144Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9628767Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9629149Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9629354Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9629866Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9630331Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9630802Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9631371Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9632108Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9632591Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9633147Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9633771Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9634464Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9635085Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9635623Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9636251Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9636931Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9637386Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9637592Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9637939Z

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9663270Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9668518Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9669068Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9669426Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9669769Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9670532Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9670978Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9671330Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9672106Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9672505Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9672981Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9673474Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9673823Z         """
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9674253Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9674860Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9675175Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9675293Z         try:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9675728Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9676283Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9676702Z             return True
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9677079Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9677636Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9678283Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9678722Z             return False
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9678928Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9679053Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9679418Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9680039Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9680489Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9686654Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9689316Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9689573Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9689762Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9690196Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9690536Z         The result should be:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9690872Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9691086Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9691310Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9691879Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9692179Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9692732Z         """
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9693073Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9693765Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9694375Z         return new_list
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9694575Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9694721Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9695159Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9695391Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9695624Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9696163Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9696746Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9697215Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9697441Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9697622Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9698041Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9698542Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9698941Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9699168Z         """
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9699417Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9699716Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9699855Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9699966Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9700388Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9700801Z             return False
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9700940Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9701040Z         # find next layout
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9701322Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9701783Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9702167Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9702571Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9702886Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9702994Z         try:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9703386Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9703936Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9704413Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9704813Z             return True
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9705199Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9705814Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9706434Z             return False

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9796628Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9796837Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9796974Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9796978Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9797078Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9797230Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9797331Z        -> return these
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9797694Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9797834Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9797989Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9798276Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9798568Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9798745Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9798755Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9799051Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9799217Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9799435Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9799598Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9799825Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9799977Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9800192Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9800345Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9800477Z     """
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9800624Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9801015Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9801168Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9801175Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9801439Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9801983Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9802148Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9802438Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9802649Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9802665Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9802804Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9803166Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9803300Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9803307Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9803464Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9804227Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9804238Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9804380Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9804390Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9804394Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9804981Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9805131Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9805361Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9805714Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9805848Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9805979Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9806199Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9806504Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9806640Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9806646Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9806784Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9864851Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9870403Z         in
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9870663Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9870922Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9871260Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9871502Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9871891Z     esac

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9875193Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9875467Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9875862Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9876185Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9876424Z     #
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9876881Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9877166Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9877521Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9877845Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9878097Z     else
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9878676Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9879269Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9879400Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9879801Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9880091Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9880379Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9880635Z       fi
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9880881Z     fi
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9881010Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9881324Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9881895Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9882047Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9882690Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9882992Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then

2026-10-24T18:32:13.9886191Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9886784Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9887227Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9887484Z     timeout=60
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9887745Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9887872Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9888191Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9888484Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9888904Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9889154Z             exit 1
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9889395Z         fi
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9889644Z         sleep 1
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9889918Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9890165Z     done
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9890293Z
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9890850Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9891360Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9892076Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9892535Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9893037Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9893362Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9893615Z     else
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9894013Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9894280Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9894520Z     fi
2026-10-24T18:32:13.9894763Z }

2026-10-24T18:32:14.0012807Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim

2026-10-24T18:32:14.0013098Z

2026-10-24T18:32:14.0022640Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0022907Z action() {
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0023148Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0023255Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0023461Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0023721Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0023925Z     shift
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0024148Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0024393Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0024596Z     else
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0024932Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0025168Z     fi
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0025382Z     rc=$?
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0025959Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0026248Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0026478Z }
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0026589Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0027000Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0027267Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0027702Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0027965Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0028272Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0028553Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0028878Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0029164Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0029598Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0029893Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0030298Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0030577Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0030927Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0031191Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0031499Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0031963Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0032081Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0032344Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0032725Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0033037Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0033147Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0033396Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0033633Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0033877Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0034109Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0034335Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0034572Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0034824Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0035052Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0035303Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0035536Z         ;;
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0035762Z esac
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0035876Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0036145Z # Log that something succeeded

2026-10-24T18:32:14.0036905Z     return 0
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0037127Z }
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0037239Z

2026-10-24T18:32:14.0038540Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0038768Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0038988Z }
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0039100Z

2026-10-24T18:32:14.0040965Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0041300Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0041545Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0046321Z     return 0
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0046550Z }
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0046666Z

2026-10-24T18:32:14.0223180Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0223424Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0223697Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0223972Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0224249Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0224465Z   fi
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0224587Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0224936Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0225235Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0225654Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0226457Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0226753Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0227103Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0227577Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0227835Z     fi
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0228136Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0228389Z   fi
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0228734Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0228875Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0229300Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0229618Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0229859Z     echo
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0230166Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0230667Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0230961Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0231215Z   fi
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0231848Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0232259Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0232514Z   fi
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0232643Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0232943Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0233273Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0233670Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0234067Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0234400Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0234809Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0235131Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0235385Z }
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0235513Z
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0235818Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0236096Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0236365Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0236737Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0237060Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0237311Z fi
2026-10-24T18:32:14.0237439Z
